L2151: Figure 32 is a line graph showing the Geometric mean (\mu\text{g/mL}) of Tralokinumab serum concentrations over 52 weeks for five different treatment groups. The Y-axis ranges from 0 to 125 \mu\text{g/mL}, and the X-axis represents Week (0 to 52).
The legend identifies the groups:
Tralokinumab Q2W (Initial) (n=602)
Tralokinumab Q2W (Maintenance) (n=68)
Tralokinumab Q4W (Maintenance) (n=76)
Tralokinumab Q2W - Tralokinumab Q2W + optional TCS (open-label, transferred at W16) (n=361)
Placebo - Tralokinumab Q2W + optional TCS (open-label, transferred at W16) (n=145)
The graph illustrates that the Tralokinumab Q2W (Initial) group achieved the highest peak concentration (around 125 \mu\text{g/mL}) around Week 16, followed by a rapid decline. The Tralokinumab Q2W (Maintenance) and Tralokinumab Q4W (Maintenance) groups maintained concentrations around 75-80 \mu\text{g/mL} until Week 16, after which concentrations dropped significantly. The Tralokinumab Q2W + optional TCS group maintained concentrations around 75-80 \mu\text{g/mL} until Week 16, after which concentrations dropped significantly. The Placebo group maintained concentrations around 50 \mu\text{g/mL} throughout the study.
L2152: Line graph showing Mean Serum Concentrations of Tralokinumab (Geometric mean in ug/mL) over 52 Weeks for five different treatment groups. The Y-axis ranges from 0 to 125 ug/mL, and the X-axis ranges from 0 to 52 Weeks. The groups are: Tralokinumab Q2W (Initial) (n=592), Tralokinumab Q2W (Maintenance) (n=91), Tralokinumab Q4W (Maintenance) (n=89), Tralokinumab Q2W - Tralokinumab Q2W + optional TCS (open-label, transferred at W16) (n=325), and Placebo - Tralokinumab Q2W + optional TCS (open-label, transferred at W16) (n=144). The Q2W Initial group shows the highest concentration, peaking around 105 ug/mL at Week 16. The Q2W Maintenance group shows a sharp decline after Week 16, dropping to approximately 45 ug/mL by Week 28. The Q4W Maintenance group maintains a concentration around 90 ug/mL. The Q2W + optional TCS group maintains a concentration around 85 ug/mL. The Placebo group maintains a concentration around 80 ug/mL.
L2153: Figure 34 is a line graph showing the Geometric Mean Serum Concentration of Tralokinumab (\mu\text{g/mL}) over time (Weeks 0 to 52) for various treatment groups. The Y-axis ranges from 0 to 125 \mu\text{g/mL}, and the X-axis ranges from 0 to 52 Weeks.
The legend identifies the following groups:
Tralokinumab Q2W + TCS (Initial) (n=252)
Tralokinumab Q2W + TCS - Tralokinumab Q2W + TCS (Responder) (n=69)
Tralokinumab Q2W + TCS - Tralokinumab Q4W + TCS (Responder) (n=69)

Placebo + TCS - Tralokinumab Q2W + TCS (attended Week 32) (n=72)
The graph illustrates that the Q2W + TCS group maintained the highest serum concentrations, peaking around Week 16 at approximately 85 \mu\text{g/mL}. The Q4W + TCS group peaked around Week 16 at approximately 90 \mu\text{g/mL}. Both groups showed a decline in concentration after Week 16. The Placebo + TCS group maintained a low concentration, around 50 \mu\text{g/mL}, throughout the study period.
L2154: Diagram illustrating the Population Pharmacokinetics Model for Tralokinumab. The model consists of three compartments: Depot compartment, Central compartment (V_2), and Peripheral compartment (V_3).
SC administration (Subcutaneous) leads to the Depot compartment.
IV administration (Intravenous) leads directly to the Central compartment (V_2).
From the Depot compartment, drug moves to the Central compartment via absorption, characterized by bioavailability (F) and absorption rate constant (k_a).
The Central compartment (V_2) is connected to the Peripheral compartment (V_3) via inter-compartmental clearance (Q).
The Central compartment (V_2) is also connected to clearance (CL).
L2155: Figure 36 displays four panels illustrating the Goodness-of-Fit Plot of the Final Model for tralokinumab PK. The plots compare observed concentrations against predicted concentrations and conditional weighted residuals (CWRES) against population predictions and time.
Top Left: Individual predictions versus concentrations. Shows Observed concentration (Y-axis) versus Individual predicted concentration (X-axis). The data points generally follow the diagonal line (Observed = Predicted), indicating good fit for individual predictions.
Top Right: Population predictions versus concentrations. Shows Observed concentration (Y-axis) versus Population predicted concentration (X-axis). The data points generally follow the diagonal line (Observed = Predicted), indicating good fit for population predictions.
Bottom Left: Conditional weighted residuals versus population predictions. Shows CWRES (Y-axis) versus Population predicted concentration (X-axis). The residuals are randomly scattered around the horizontal line at CWRES = 0, indicating no systematic bias in the population predictions.
Bottom Right: Conditional weighted residuals versus time. Shows CWRES (Y-axis) versus Time after dose (X-axis). The residuals are randomly scattered around the horizontal line at CWRES = 0, indicating no systematic bias related to time.
In all plots, black circles represent individual observations/predictions/conditional weighted residuals, and the red line is the trend line (LOESS).
L2156: Box plot showing AUC from week 14 to 16 (in µg*day/mL) versus weight quartiles (Q1: 37-63 kg, Q2: 63-74 kg, Q3: 74-86 kg, Q4: 86-165 kg). The AUC generally increases with weight quartile, with Q4 showing the highest median and range.
L2157: Figure 38 is a line graph showing Tralokinumab concentration (µg/mL) on the Y-axis versus Time (weeks) on the X-axis. The X-axis ranges from 0 to 52 weeks, marked every 4 weeks. The Y-axis ranges from 0 to 100 µg/mL, marked every 30 µg/mL. Two lines are plotted: a solid blue line and a dashed yellow line. Both lines show a rapid increase in concentration from 0 to approximately 4 weeks, reaching a plateau around 100 µg/mL by Week 8. The concentration remains stable and oscillating slightly around 100 µg/mL from Week 16 onwards. A vertical line is drawn at Week 16, indicating the time to reach steady state.
L2158: Figure 39 displays two concentration-time profiles (Tralokinumab concentration in \mu\text{g/mL} vs. Time in weeks) for subjects with typical bodyweights (111 kg and 72 kg) following varying dosing regimens (Q2W and Q4W).
The Y-axis represents Tralokinumab concentration (\mu\text{g/mL}), ranging from 0 to 100. The X-axis represents Time (weeks), ranging from 0 to 52.
Top Panel: Shows the concentration-time profiles for a typical subject (weight 111 kg, solid blue line) following tralokinumab 300 mg Q2W for 52 weeks, and another typical subject (weight 72 kg, yellow dashed line) following tralokinumab 300 mg Q2W for 16 weeks followed by tralokinumab 300 mg Q4W until Week 52. The blue line shows sustained high concentrations (around 70-80 \mu\text{g/mL}) throughout the 52 weeks. The yellow dashed line shows high concentrations (around 90-100 \mu\text{g/mL}) for the first 16 weeks, followed by a sharp decline and sustained low concentrations (around 30-40 \mu\text{g/mL}) for the remaining 36 weeks.
Bottom Panel: Shows the concentration-time profile for a typical subject (weight 111 kg, solid blue line) and another typical subject (weight 72 kg, yellow dashed line) both dosed with tralokinumab 300 mg Q2W for 16 weeks followed by tralokinumab 300 mg Q4W until Week 52. The blue line shows sustained high concentrations (around 70-80 \mu\text{g/mL}) throughout the 52 weeks. The yellow dashed line shows high concentrations (around 90-100 \mu\text{g/mL}) for the first 16 weeks, followed by a sharp decline and sustained low concentrations (around 30-40 \mu\text{g/mL}) for the remaining 36 weeks.
L2159: Figure 40 displays six plots showing EASI and IGA responses stratified by quartiles of Tralokinumab exposure (Ctrough, AUC, and Weight) over 16 weeks. The plots compare Placebo (solid blue line) against four exposure quartiles (Q1, Q2, Q3, Q4, represented by dashed lines with different markers).
The left column plots show the percentage change from baseline (EASI) over time (weeks). The right column plots show the percentage of IGA 0/1 responders over time (weeks).
The plots are arranged in three rows, corresponding to the stratification variables:
Top Row: Stratified by Ctrough quartiles.
Middle Row: Stratified by AUC quartiles.
Bottom Row: Stratified by Weight quartiles.
In all plots, tralokinumab treatment (Q1-Q4) shows significantly greater reduction in EASI score and higher percentage of IGA 0/1 responders compared to Placebo, and generally, responses increase with increasing quartile number (Q1 < Q2 < Q3 < Q4).
L2160: Figure 41 displays two pairs of plots showing the logistic regression of the probability of achieving EASI-75 (Left) and IGA (0,1) (Right) versus tralokinumab trough concentration at Week 16 (Conc at week 16 (\mu\text{g/mL})) in patients with Moderate-to-Severe AD.
The top row shows the probability curves. The left plot shows Probability achieving EASI-75 (Y-axis, 0.00 to 1.00) versus Conc at week 16 (\mu\text{g/mL}) (X-axis, 0 to 300). The right plot shows Probability achieving IGA 0,1 (Y-axis, 0.00 to 1.00) versus Conc at week 16 (\mu\text{g/mL}) (X-axis, 0 to 300). Both plots show a sigmoid curve representing the predicted mean probability (black line) and the 95% confidence interval (grey shaded area). The p-values for the regression lines are P=1.83\text{e}-05 for EASI-75 and P=2.92\text{e}-05 for IGA 0,1, indicating statistical significance.
The bottom row shows the quartile analysis. The left plot shows Quartile (Q1, Q2, Q3, Q4) on the Y-axis versus Conc at week 16 (\mu\text{g/mL}) on the X-axis. The right plot shows Quartile (Q1, Q2, Q3, Q4) on the Y-axis versus Conc at week 16 (\mu\text{g/mL}) on the X-axis. These plots display the means of response variables (black circles) and confidence intervals (black vertical lines) for each quartile of exposure.

L2170: Line graph showing IGA Success (IGA 0 or 1) Response Rate (%) over 16 to 32 weeks for three groups: Tralokinumab Q2W (N=45), Tralokinumab Q4W (N=36), and Placebo (N=33). The Y-axis ranges from 0 to 100%. At Week 16, all groups start at 100% response rate. By Week 18, Tralokinumab Q2W is around 88%, Tralokinumab Q4W is around 70%, and Placebo is around 78%. By Week 32, Tralokinumab Q2W is around 88%, Tralokinumab Q4W is around 78%, and Placebo is around 60%. The Placebo group shows the steepest decline in response rate after Week 18.
L2171: Line graph showing EASI-75 Response Rate (%) over time (Week) for the Continuation Treatment Period. The X-axis represents Week (16, 18, 20, 22, 24, 26, 28, 30, 32). The Y-axis represents Response Rate (%), ranging from 0 to 100. Three treatment groups are plotted: Tralokinumab Q2W (N=65, red triangles), Tralokinumab Q4W (N=62, green diamonds), and Placebo (N=40, blue circles). All groups start at 100% response rate at Week 16. The Placebo group shows a significant drop in response rate, reaching approximately 78% at Week 32. The Tralokinumab groups maintain high response rates, fluctuating between 85% and 95% across the weeks shown.
